Diagnosed via audit-vs-DB comparison; a one-character content touch forced recompile and everything works. Documented as a dev-mode hazard: after editing server libs, verify behavior, not just compilation.

Header comment documents the full release flow (bump version → tag desktop-vX.Y.Z → push → apps self-update).

- Files created (8): desktop/package.json, desktop/main.js, desktop/preload.js, desktop/prepare-server.mjs, desktop/README.md, desktop/resources/icon.png, .github/workflows/desktop-release.yml, README.md (root). Files modified (2): .gitignore (appended desktop block), eslint.config.mjs (added desktop/** ignore — see deviation above).
- The Windows app is a thin, robust shell around the existing platform: same standalone Next server, same SQLite seed (download/rsm-platform-database.db staged by CI), per-PC data + secrets under %APPDATA%/rsm-desktop, and electron-updater pointing at the public repo's Releases (NSIS channel auto-updates; portable exe is a download-only convenience).
- CI risks (specific, for the lead): (1) First-run unknown — the full chain (bun install on windows-latest → bunx next build with Turbopack → prepare-server → electron-builder NSIS publish) has never executed; watch the first desktop-v* run. (2) Electron 31 embeds Node 20.14/20.15 — Next 16's floor is 20.9, satisfied, but if the standalone runtime trips on a newer Node API the fix is bumping electron ^31.7.0 → ^32/^33 in desktop/package.json (no code change). (3) `--frozen-lockfile` + sharp/@prisma optional native deps on Windows should resolve from bun.lock, but a bun/Windows quirk would surface at install time. (4) 40-min timeout is generous but Turbopack on a 2-core windows runner is the slowest step. (5) workflow_dispatch without a tag publishes for desktop/package.json's version — if that release already exists the publish step can conflict; tag pushes are the canonical flow. (6) Releases created with the per-run GITHUB_TOKEN do not trigger other workflows (by GitHub design — harmless here). (7) SmartScreen will always warn on the unsigned exe — documented in the README ("More info → Run anyway"); a code-signing cert can be added later via standard CSC_* secrets without config changes.
- Release runbook (for the lead): commit these files → push → tag desktop-v1.0.0 → push tag → CI publishes Setup + Portable + latest.yml; installed apps then update themselves on the 30-min check + next quit. Keep desktop/package.json "version" and the tag suffix in sync (artifacts/latest.yml are versioned from package.json).

(6) The orders POST reservation auto-link updateMany stays fire-and-forget unwired (map scope). (8) The local device identity created during the check (d70fae86…) intentionally REMAINS — it is the installation's legitimate identity state, not test data; audit rows from the two runs also remain (append-only audit trail — deleting audit history would be worse than leaving it).

Removed ONLY my own broken copy (documented in backups/BACKUP-NOTES.md); zero user/production data touched.
- HONEST FINDINGS (reported, NOT unilaterally changed — additive-only directive): (F1) TOCTOU race on the overpay guard — payments route reads paidAmount OUTSIDE the write tx; two terminals paying the same open check concurrently can overshoot the bill (low-medium severity; single-dev-DB SQLite narrows the window, Neon cloud widens it). (F2) Loyalty redemption commits in its OWN tx before the payments tx — a failure of the second leaves points burned + loyalty tender row without the cash/card rows (partial-failure inconsistency, low probability). (F3) Transfer-items after partial payment can leave the SOURCE overpaid (paid > recomputed total; stays open, remaining clamped 0 in UI) — manual close route is the escape hatch, but there is no overpaid-delta refund prompt and transfer does not call closeOrderIfFullyPaid on the source. (F4) Upscale gap: no per-payer itemized sub-check PRINTING from the payment flow (By-Items prints payer totals on the shared paper; the standalone check modal supports item-selected sub-checks but the payment modal does not offer "print N checks, one per payer").

Stage Summary:
- SPLIT-CHECK WORKFLOW FULLY AUDITED: 32/32 live API checks PASS + browser E2E of all 4 split modes (single w/ R26 tender flow, equal, by-items, custom via code path) + printed split check + split receipt + refund + Z-report + drawer math + hybrid outbox + legacy sync + desktop offline path — every layer mapped, wired, connected, implemented. i18n EN/AR complete. No product bug found in the golden paths.
- 4 findings reported for the roadmap (F1 race hardening, F2 tx folding, F3 overpaid-transfer UX, F4 per-payer check printing) — none blocking, none fixed without approval per additive-only rule.
- DATA SAFETY: pre-audit + VACUUM-INTO post-audit backups verified (integrity ok, exact row match); zero deletions; backups documented in backups/BACKUP-NOTES.md.

- LATENT BUG FOUND (honest): the 3 functions were registered on Inngest Cloud with EMPTY triggers — they existed but could NEVER fire. Root cause: src/inngest/functions.ts used the v3 shorthand createFunction({ id, cron }, …); inngest@4.21.0's _createFunction reads ONLY rawOptions.triggers (sanitizeTriggers(rawOptions.triggers)) — the top-level cron key is silently ignored. Proof: the endpoint's own in-band registry (signed PUT, x-inngest-sync-kind: in_band) returned triggers:[] for all 3. This bug predates the integration detach (functions registered trigger-less since creation) — masked all along by the Vercel cron mirrors, which is why business functions kept running and nothing visibly broke.
- FIX (additive, 3 lines): createFunction({ id, triggers: [{ cron }] }, …) for all 3 — schedules unchanged (30 3 * * *, 30 6 * * *, */30 * * * *). Verified locally: bun script invoking the actual serve() handlers with the signing key → in-band registry now returns all triggers. bunx tsc clean (no new errors); bun run lint 0 findings.
- DEPLOYED: c733739 pushed → Vercel auto-deploy wedjatrsm-lna75dqy5 READY → signed out-of-band sync ping (PUT /api/inngest with HMAC t/s signature over body+ts, key prefix stripped — scheme read from the SDK source) → {"message":"Successfully registered","modified":true}; prod in-band registry now serves all 3 triggers.
- PLATFORM VERIFIED: Inngest Cloud API now shows rsm-turso-replica-sync [cron 30 3 * * *], rsm-daily-digest [cron 30 6 * * *], rsm-stale-order-alert [cron */30 * * * *] — all with triggers.

- FIX 1 (CRITICAL, payment-modal.tsx): submitRows + checkRows memos were missing cashCharge/singleCash deps — partial cash tender submitted the STALE full-bill amount → 400 on every partial cash payment. Fixed deps; verified live (300 → "remaining EGP 158.18").
- FIX 2 (cart-panel.tsx): payment button showed displayTotals.total after partial payments — now shows remainingAmount once paidAmount > 0.
- FIX 3 (CRITICAL, src/lib/db.ts): SQLite journal_mode was 'delete' → write convoy + read starvation. Now WAL (persistent in header, boot-applied, gated to file: URLs) + synchronous=NORMAL. NOTE: busy_timeout PRAGMA intentionally NOT set — it overrides the URL socket_timeout (measured).
- FIX 4 (CRITICAL, orders.ts + orders/route.ts + delivery webhook): recomputeTotals(orderId, txScope?) — the create transactions now fold the money write atomically (zombie zero-total orders impossible); 30 s tx timeout + 15 s maxWait; withWriteLock (NEW src/lib/write-mutex.ts, FIFO promise-chain mutex) around both create transactions.
- ROOT-CAUSE CHASE (the hard one): 8 concurrent creates hung 60-90 s on a FRESH process. Trace instrumentation proved: first tx commits fine, a later tx hangs mid-transaction holding the SQLite write lock; the hybrid engine's apply-transaction busy-waits on that lock INSIDE the Prisma runtime, blocking the runtime the first tx needs → circular deadlock. Two environment traps found: (a) the platform shell exports a PARAM-LESS DATABASE_URL that silently beats .env (Next never overrides inherited process.env) — fixed in .zscripts/daemonize-dev.py (NEW python double-fork daemonizer, strips the inherited var, survives the sandbox reaper) + ensure-dev.sh; (b) PRAGMA busy_timeout after connect overrides the URL param. FINAL FIX STACK: WAL + ?socket_timeout=30000&connection_limit=1 (DATABASE_URL, set in .env AND vault) + withWriteLock + 30 s tx timeout.
- FINAL STRESS GATE: 10,781 reqs / 0 ERRORS. Write path: 695 creates + 695 cancels in 45 s (15.4/s sustained, create p50 222 ms p99 905 ms — was 93% failure). Reads: 95-113 rps, p95 ≤ 232 ms. Mixed phase: reads p95 242 ms under write load (was 5.2 s). INTEGRITY: 7/7 PASS — 831 created test orders ALL cancelled with exact money math, ZERO zombies (atomic-create fix proven at scale), 206 pre-existing orders intact, no new dead outbox letters.
- DATA REPAIRS (designed channels, all audit-logged + outbox-synced): cancelled 13 stale Sept-28 load-test takeaways + 3 zero-total zombies via POST cancel API; recomputed order #40 (225/283.50 → 485/611.10, closing the EGP 327.60 undercharge risk on table 1); normalized order #87 takeaway→dinein (legacy pre-guard row bound to table 2). Open orders 23 → 10 (all legitimate); POS floor + KDS now clean.
- FIX 5 (cancel route): developer role now admin-equivalent for cancellations (was manual role!=='admin' check); merged orders can no longer be cancelled (400, merge-trail protection).
- FIX 6 (db.ts logging): dev query logging now OPT-IN via RSM_QUERY_LOG=1 (measured: 70k lines / 23 MB per 3-min stress run; server ballooned to 2.5 GB RSS and was OOM-killed once mid-audit).
- OPS: dev server OOM'd twice during the audit (stress residue + query logging on 4 GB box) — recovered via the new daemonizer each time; final state healthy, lint CLEAN, tsc clean on all touched files.
- REPORT: docs/r31-coo-audit.md — full findings register (5 CRITICAL all fixed, 2 HIGH dispositioned, 6 MEDIUM fixed/documented, LOW/INFO notes), before/after stress tables, security posture, restaurant-ops assessment + 6 prioritized recommendations (stale-order guard, PUT validation helper, payment-row reconciliation for cloud-pulled orders, account-level check chip, data-doctor tool, stress-as-release-gate).
- RUNBOOK: r31 entry added to the fixes ledger + new §6.11 environment rules (URL params mandatory, never PRAGMA busy_timeout, withWriteLock convention, query-log opt-in, daemonizer usage).

Stage Summary:
- The platform's security, money math, and feature depth were verified EXCELLENT (0 bypasses in 364 probes; exact tax/service math everywhere; all 22 modules + bilingual + mobile + RTL clean) — but the audit proved the order-write path could NOT survive a real Friday rush (93% create failure at 6 concurrent waiters) and corrupted data while failing (zombie zero-total orders synced to Neon). Both are now fixed and stress-proven: 10,781 requests, 0 errors, 15.4 creates/sec sustained, zero data loss, 7/7 integrity checks.
- Four-layer write-path fix: WAL + socket_timeout=30000 + connection_limit=1 (URL, vault-durable) + withWriteLock FIFO mutex + atomic create with 30 s tx timeout. Two environment traps documented forever in the RUNBOOK (inherited DATABASE_URL beats .env; post-connect PRAGMA overrides URL params).
- Data hygiene restored: 16 stale/zombie orders cancelled, the EGP 327.60 undercharge on table 1 closed, order #87 normalized — every repair through designed, audit-logged, sync-riding channels. 22 historical payment-less paid orders documented (never fabricated) with a reconciliation recommendation.
- 44 browser screenshots + 2 subagent reports (agent-ctx/r31-*) + reusable stress/integrity gates (scripts/r31-*) + the full COO report (docs/r31-coo-audit.md) are the durable artifacts of this round.

- ROOT CAUSE (Arabic out of border) — three stacked defects in the print-window CSS shared by check-modal + receipt-modal: (1) `body{width:320px;padding:24px}` with NO box-sizing → a 368px document spilling past the popup viewport and past 80mm thermal paper (~302px @96dpi); (2) `font-family:monospace` — Courier has NO Arabic glyphs, so the browser silently fell back to arbitrary system fonts with wider metrics; (3) `.r` flex rows had no gap/wrap rules — long bilingual labels ("Service tax · ضريبة الخدمة (12%)") pushed amounts off the sheet; `.ar` lines had no overflow-wrap for long Arabic words.
- FIX A — shared `paperCss()` in src/lib/print.ts (both papers now share ONE stylesheet): border-box everywhere, fixed sheet (320px screen / 74mm print), Arabic-capable font stack ('Courier New' for Latin, Tahoma/Segoe UI/Arial for Arabic runs), label span wraps (`overflow-wrap:anywhere` + `min-width:0`) while the amount stays pinned (`nowrap` + tabular-nums), Arabic lines wrap, `@page{size:80mm auto;margin:3mm}` + `@media print{body{width:74mm}}` for thermal stock (A4 scales/centers the same sheet), print-color-adjust:exact for the stamps.
- FIX B (bilingual check) — the paper was already EN·AR for labels/titles/items; closed the gap: selected options (modifiers) now print in BOTH languages on the check (mods + modsAr sub-lines, mirroring the receipt — previously UI-language only).
- FIX C (cashier holder shows the waiter who served) — the old single "Waiter" row showed the CHECK ISSUER (cashier) — the two roles were blurred together. Now BOTH papers print two attribution rows: "Waiter · النادل" = order.user (the account that took/served the order) and "Cashier · الكاشير" = checkIssuedByPerson → session person → creating account. ReceiptModal now receives sessionPersonName (pos-view passes user.personName to both instances). New i18n key pos.cashier (EN/AR).
- BONUS FIX — src/lib/print.ts had a DEAD broken import (`STATIONS`/`STATION_LABELS` never existed in constants.ts — the R11 refactor only created PREP_STATION_*; nothing imported the module so the broken link never fired). Now that the check/receipt papers import paperCss from this module, the station vocabulary is defined locally — module safe to import.
- Preview hardening: on-screen thermal previews (CheckRow/ReceiptRow) had shrink-0 labels that could push amounts out of the card — labels now wrap within 68%, Arabic preview lines break-words.
- E2E VERIFICATION (live service — order 1214 table 1 active during the whole session, untouched): created designed test takeaway order #1215 (Cheese Fries + Omelet Cheesy/Extra Cheese — exercises Arabic item names + bilingual modifiers) → check modal → paper verified 3 ways: (a) EN-UI paper: 320px sheet, 0 elements overflowing the border; (b) AR-UI RTL paper (dir=rtl): 0 overflow, both rows; (c) BEFORE/AFTER proof at 80mm paper width: old CSS = 392px doc clipped 90px past the 302px paper; new CSS = fits exactly, 0 overflow. VLM-verified screenshots (Arabic fully inside border on all papers).
- WAITER ≠ CASHIER PROVEN LIVE: printed the check as person اميره (Meina's account, person picker) on an order created by Developer → paper + DB stamp both show Waiter = Developer (served), Cashier = اميره (issued). The check-issue POST stamped اميره (first-stamp-wins).
- RECEIPT FLOW VERIFIED: paid order #1215 cash (EGP 346.50 exact, recipe→stock deducted 8 ingredient moves incl. 25g cheese), receipt paper printed: 0 overflow, PAID stamp, Waiter + Cashier rows, bilingual throughout. VLM-verified.
- CLEANUP (p21-b precedent): payment refunded to net zero through the designed refund API (reason documented) — order #1215 stays paid+refunded like #1213; the one-serving ingredient deduction remains and is documented here. No live orders touched; live takeaway list back to pre-test state.
- QUALITY: lint clean; tsc clean on all touched files (src/components + src/lib zero errors — the scripts/ legacy errors are pre-existing and documented). Dev server healthy throughout (no errors in dev.log).
- DEPLOYED: commit a60b6e3 pushed to GitHub main → Vercel auto-deploy wedjatrsm-m5693bbel-tonsy.vercel.app READY; prod smoke GET / 200 in 0.79s. 13 screenshots in screenshots/p22/.

Stage Summary:
- The printed check (and receipt) now keep EVERY line — Arabic included — inside the paper border on any paper: the shared paperCss enforces a fixed sheet with wrapping rules and an Arabic-capable font stack, with 80mm thermal print rules (74mm body inside 3mm margins).
- Both papers are fully bilingual EN + AR (labels, titles, item names, options, stamps, footer); numbers stay Latin digits by design.
- Attribution is now honest and standard restaurant practice: Waiter = who served the table, Cashier = who printed/collected — visible on the paper, stamped in the DB (first stamp wins), and synced.
- Fixed a latent module-level landmine (print.ts's dead STATIONS import) that my shared-CSS refactor would have tripped.

  1. ORIGIN-FIRST WAS HALF-BROKEN: an exe downloaded from the local/preview instance synced with that origin, but a local SQLite origin NEVER serves its own POS writes on its pull stream (they ride the engine→cloud outbox) — the agent would never see the owner's changes. FIX (agent v1.1): HUB-FIRST config — baseUrl = the durable cloud hub (Vercel/Neon), the download origin becomes the failover.
  2. LOCAL-HUB ECHO MISSING: a device event applied on a local instance stayed local-only (never reached Neon). FIX (server): POST /api/hybrid/push now re-emits every landed write as an outbox event on the local SQLite data plane, so the engine carries it to the hub. Cloud data plane (Vercel) skips the echo (the apply IS the cloud write).
- AGENT v1.1 FIXES (agent-desktop/main.ts): hub-first download-time config (+ v1.0 config auto-migration), proper primary/fallback failover toggle (v1.0 could get stuck on the fallback forever), 401-credential-rejection → automatic re-enrollment against the current server (each instance keeps its own device registry), gateway-aware dashboard fetch (viewable in the sandbox preview at /?XTransformPort=9753), version 1.0.0 → 1.1.0.

Task ID: r38-6a
Agent: full-stack-developer (emit-wiring)
Task: Wire outbox emitters into the 14 reference/config CRUD routes

Work Log:
- Read worklog.md global conventions + hybrid-sync core (outbox.ts contract, entity-policy.ts registry, apply-remote-event.ts delete handling, push.ts wire format — confirmed rsm-hybrid/1 DOES carry delete operations verbatim) + the two canonical wired routes (tables/[id], customers) before touching anything; then read all 14 target route files in full.
- Wiring pattern applied consistently: business write + emitOutboxEvent in the SAME db.$transaction; the emitted `row` is always the PLAIN Prisma row (raw columns only, no include relations — matches the reservations/payments precedent); where a route previously created/updated WITH an include for its response, the tx now writes plain + emits + the response row is re-read with the include afterwards (response shapes byte-identical).
- products/route.ts POST → Product 'create' (nested initial-stock InventoryTransaction + ProductModifierGroup links deliberately NOT emitted per task spec; link table is outside the registry).
- products/[id]/route.ts PUT → Product 'update' (ModifierGroup relink not emitted, per spec note); DELETE (soft) → Product 'delete' with pre-write snapshot (read full row first, then the active=false write, then emit).
- categories/route.ts POST → Category 'create' (write wrapped in new $transaction); categories/[id]/route.ts PUT → Category 'update' (plain update in tx, response re-read with _count); DELETE (soft) → Category 'delete' pre-write snapshot.
- modifier-groups/route.ts POST → ModifierGroup 'create' + Modifier 'create' for every nested option (options re-read via tx.modifier.findMany inside the tx); response/audit row re-read with GROUP_INCLUDE.
- modifier-groups/[id]/route.ts PUT → ModifierGroup 'update' + full Modifier diff events: doomed options snapshotted BEFORE deleteMany → 'delete' each; per-option update → 'update'; new options → 'create'. DELETE soft path (?soft=1) → ModifierGroup 'delete' pre-write snapshot; DELETE hard path → ModifierGroup 'delete' + Modifier 'delete' for every cascade-removed option (group + options snapshotted inside the tx before the cascade delete).
- recipes/route.ts POST (upsert on productId+ingredientId) → pre-upsert findUnique distinguishes create vs update → RecipeComponent 'create'/'update' with the plain upserted row; response re-read with ingredient include.
- promotions/route.ts POST → Promotion 'create' (plain create in tx, re-read with PROMO_INCLUDE for audit+response); promotions/[id]/route.ts PUT → Promotion 'update' (same pattern); DELETE → Promotion 'delete' pre-delete snapshot inside tx.
- stock-counts/route.ts POST → StockCount 'create' + StockCountLine 'create' for EVERY snapshot line (lines re-read via tx.stockCountLine.findMany in the tx; ~N events for N stockable products — the designed per-line registry granularity); response sheet re-read with SC_INCLUDE.
- suppliers/route.ts POST → Supplier 'create' (plain create in tx; response re-read with PO aggregates). File has only GET+POST — the PUT/DELETE live in suppliers/[id]/route.ts which is OUTSIDE the 14-file scope.
- purchase-orders/route.ts POST → PurchaseOrder 'create' + PurchaseOrderItem 'create' for each line (tx.purchaseOrderItem.findMany inside the tx); response PO re-read with PO_INCLUDE.
- roles/route.ts POST → CustomRole 'create' (plain create in tx; P2002→409 catch preserved around the $transaction; response re-read with _count). roles/[id]/route.ts PUT → CustomRole 'update' (same); DELETE → CustomRole 'delete' pre-delete snapshot inside tx. Updated the stale "rsm-hybrid/1 carries no delete events" NB comment (push.ts sends operation verbatim + apply-remote-event applies deletes) — it directly contradicted the new emission.
- Entity names exactly per registry; nothing emitted for User or ProductModifierGroup anywhere; no validation/auth/serialization/response-shape changes; no files outside the 14 routes + the verify script touched.
- Wrote scripts/r38/verify-emit-wiring.ts (source-level check: splits each file into exported handler blocks, asserts every expected write handler emits, verifies emitted entity sets against the expected map, flags non-registry entity names, catch-all for any other writing handler): 19/19 handler checks ALL OK.
- LIVE SMOKE PROOF (engine connected to cloud, sync not paused): no-op value-preserving PUTs → outbox events #11738 Category/28 update (the exact repro from the mission — a category displayOrder change), #11739 Product/3 update, #11740 ModifierGroup/1 update — all with clean raw-column payloads (no _count/relations), all went pending → ACKED by the engine's own push cycle within ~30s. Local menu/config edits now flow to the cloud.
- bun run lint: exit 0, ZERO errors. bunx tsc --noEmit: zero errors in the 14 wired files + the verify script (remaining errors are the pre-existing baseline in other agents' scripts + products/reorder which is outside scope and untouched).

Stage Summary:
- Files wired (14): products/route.ts, products/[id]/route.ts, categories/route.ts, categories/[id]/route.ts, modifier-groups/route.ts, modifier-groups/[id]/route.ts, recipes/route.ts, promotions/route.ts, promotions/[id]/route.ts, stock-counts/route.ts, suppliers/route.ts, purchase-orders/route.ts, roles/route.ts, roles/[id]/route.ts — 19 write handlers, 100% coverage of registry entities written by those files (Product, Category, ModifierGroup, Modifier, RecipeComponent, Promotion, Supplier, PurchaseOrder, PurchaseOrderItem, StockCount, StockCountLine, CustomRole).
- Coverage verified by scripts/r38/verify-emit-wiring.ts (19/19 OK) + live end-to-end (3 no-op writes → events pushed & acked to the cloud). Lint 0 errors, no new tsc errors.
- OUT-OF-SCOPE GAPS found (registry-entity write paths NOT in the 14-file list, left untouched per the "no files outside the 14" rule — candidates for a follow-up wiring pass): suppliers/[id] PUT+DELETE (Supplier), stock-counts/[id] PATCH + [id]/counts PUT + [id]/post POST (StockCount/StockCountLine + InventoryTransaction/Product), purchase-orders/[id] PATCH (PurchaseOrder/PurchaseOrderItem/InventoryTransaction/Product), products/[id]/sold-out PATCH (Product), recipes/[id] DELETE (RecipeComponent — the only RecipeComponent delete path), categories/reorder + products/reorder PUTs (dead endpoints per r37 audit, no UI callers). Also noted: products POST's nested initial-stock InventoryTransaction ('purchase') is not emitted (per task spec for this file) — if cross-device inventory ledgers must be complete, it needs its own wiring decision.

Task ID: r38-6b
Agent: full-stack-developer (emit-wiring completion)
Task: Wire remaining follow-up outbox emitters (sub-routes + nested writes)

Work Log:
- Read worklog conventions + r38-6a's entry, the canonical wired references (customers, tables/[id]), promotions/[id] (re-read + pre-delete-tx patterns), purchase-orders & stock-counts POST wiring (parent-then-children emit style), the outbox contract (outbox.ts), the entity registry (entity-policy.ts) and apply-remote-event.ts (remote apply = whole-row upsert; an InventoryTransaction event alone never touches product stock — which is why the Product stock/cost writes in post/receive MUST also ride the outbox or terminal stock levels diverge). All 8 target files confirmed to exist.
- suppliers/[id]/route.ts: PUT → plain tx.supplier.update + Supplier 'update' in one $transaction, response row re-read with SUPPLIER_AGG_INCLUDE (aggregates stay out of the payload); DELETE → read-plain-row→delete→emit Supplier 'delete' pre-delete snapshot inside the tx (409 PO-guard unchanged).
- stock-counts/[id]/route.ts PATCH (cancel): plain tx.stockCount.update + StockCount 'update' in a new $transaction; response sheet re-read with SC_INCLUDE.
- stock-counts/[id]/counts/route.ts PUT: inside the existing tx, each tx.stockCountLine.update row is captured and emitted as StockCountLine 'update' (one event per counted line); the parent sheet row is not modified by this handler → no StockCount event.
- stock-counts/[id]/post/route.ts POST: inside the existing tx — InventoryTransaction 'create' per variance ledger row (append-only), Product 'update' per stock move (whole-row snapshot incl. new stock), StockCount 'update' for the terminal status flip; response sheet re-read with SC_INCLUDE. No StockCountLine writes exist in the post path.
- purchase-orders/[id]/route.ts PATCH: confirm + cancel wrapped in new $transactions (plain update + PurchaseOrder 'update', response re-read with PO_INCLUDE); receive path emits InventoryTransaction 'create' per received delta, Product 'update' per stock+cost revaluation, PurchaseOrderItem 'update' per line written (incl. zero-delta lines whose row update runs), PurchaseOrder 'update' for the final status flip — all inside the existing $transaction, plain rows only.
- products/[id]/sold-out/route.ts PATCH (86 toggle): update wrapped in $transaction + Product 'update' (full plain row incl. new soldOut); response shape unchanged.
- recipes/[id]/route.ts DELETE: read-plain-row→delete→emit RecipeComponent 'delete' pre-delete snapshot inside the tx (the only RecipeComponent delete path).
- products/route.ts POST: in addition to the wired Product 'create', the nested initial-stock ledger row now rides the outbox — when isStockable && stock>0 the InventoryTransaction rows are re-read via tx.inventoryTransaction.findMany inside the SAME tx and emitted as 'create' each; stale "not emitted" comment updated (modifier-group links still excluded — link table outside the registry).
- Entity names exactly per registry ('Supplier','StockCount','StockCountLine','PurchaseOrder','PurchaseOrderItem','Product','RecipeComponent','InventoryTransaction'); nothing emitted for User or ProductModifierGroup; no validation/auth/serialization/response-shape/behavior changes — only emissions inside the business transactions.
- Wrote scripts/r38/verify-emit-wiring-2.ts (same source-check style as r38-6a's script + a forbidden-entity check): 9/9 handler checks ALL OK; re-ran r38-6a's verify-emit-wiring.ts → still 19/19 OK (products POST enrichment is additive, no first-wave regression).
- Compile smoke: unauthenticated PUT/PATCH/POST/DELETE hits against all 8 routes → all 401 (auth guard), zero 500s; dev.log clean. bun run lint: exit 0, ZERO errors. bunx tsc --noEmit: 45 errors all in the pre-existing baseline (agent-desktop/main.ts, old scripts, products/reorder — untouched out-of-scope); zero errors in the 8 wired files + the new verify script.

Stage Summary:
- Files wired (8, all listed routes existed): suppliers/[id], stock-counts/[id], stock-counts/[id]/counts, stock-counts/[id]/post, purchase-orders/[id], products/[id]/sold-out, recipes/[id], products (nested initial-stock) — 9 write handlers, 100% coverage of registry-entity writes in those files: Supplier (update/delete), StockCount (update), StockCountLine (update per line), PurchaseOrder (update), PurchaseOrderItem (update per line), Product (update: sold-out toggle, stock-count post stock moves, PO receive stock/cost revaluation), RecipeComponent (delete), InventoryTransaction (create: stock-count adjustments, PO receipts, initial-stock purchases).
- Combined with r38-6a (19/19) this closes every registry-entity write path in the app except the two dead reorder endpoints (categories/reorder, products/reorder — no UI callers per r37 audit, left untouched per no-touch rule). Coverage verified by verify-emit-wiring-2.ts (9/9 OK) + first-wave script re-run (19/19 OK); lint 0 errors, no new tsc errors.
- Design note: Product 'update' emissions in stock-count post + PO receive are REQUIRED for cross-device stock convergence (remote apply of an InventoryTransaction event only inserts the ledger row — it never moves product stock; the Product event carries the authoritative post-write stock snapshot).

Excluded by design: User, ProductModifierGroup (composite PK), 2 dead reorder endpoints.

Stage Summary:
- Role→permissions: derived once at login (derivePermissions, src/lib/auth.ts:79-92) from BUILTIN_ROLE_PERMISSIONS (admin/developer = all 21 PERMISSIONS; waiter=[pos]; kitchen=[kitchen]; custom reads comma-list from CustomRole row, empty when the role record is inactive). Permissions ride the signed session and are re-derived fresh from the DB on EVERY request via getSessionUser→loadSessionUser (role edits apply immediately).
- UI gating is consistent in 4 places (page.tsx VIEW_PERMISSION/isViewAllowed, app-navbar MAIN_ITEMS/ADMIN_MENU filter, launcher tile filter, mobile-shell allowedViews) + hash deep-links are re-guarded (disallowed hash → fallback default view). API gating is per-route requireAuth(req, allowed) where allowed mixes legacy role names + module keys; admin and developer always pass; empty list = any session.
- Custom roles (مينا → "مدير الصاله"): wall shows canUseApp only when the comma permissions string has ≥1 entry; login → session with exactly the ticked modules; launcher/nav/views/APIs all filter on those keys only. She gets no tiles she wasn't granted (e.g. no Roles, no Activity Log, no Vision).
- Shifts are pure attendance/payroll reference windows (late minutes +15 grace, wall display, my-shift sheet name, payroll hours × rate). There is NO supervisor field, NO per-shift assignment of users, and NO code anywhere that widens a user's visibility based on being on-shift or on another user's shift. "On shift" only lights the green dot/badges. Verdict: a user on the evening (or any) shift sees exactly their role's modules — nothing more, nothing less.
- Gaps worth flagging: (1) 'users'-permission holders can create/promote admin accounts via POST/PUT /api/users (privilege escalation unless intended); (2) team-wall canUseApp ignores CustomRole.active; (3) download dialogs on the launcher are session-gated only (password is the real gate) — cosmetic; (4) order list/table status are shared across all POS users by design (cross-waiter visibility, not a role leak).
